The user approved the Spec and Plan on 2026-11-12 (EVD-013) and confirmed the exact reviewed bytes (EVD-015, EVD-016).

#### Change Contract Classification

Added 2026-11-14 under SPEC-0213 (W03-D77, PKG W00:822-831). One row for every open row of `Residue Handoff` as read before this package's closure and cell edits (76 rows, equal to the 76 open ledger entries), for the six seeds that this package adds, and for each other package in the tree. The class names are analysis labels only (W00:824); none is a registry state or a lifecycle state. Only `CANCEL_OBSOLETE` is defined by the PKG (W00:828: a requirement the user removed, never a PASS); the other four are this Spec's operational reading for residue rows: `KEEP_VALID` the obligation is valid and its owner is right, `REVISE` the valid part is kept and a cell is corrected, `SUPERSEDE` a new owner replaces it, `REMOVE_AFTER_TRANSFER` the obligation is closed or transferred and its entry leaves the ledger. In the PKG (W00:827) `REMOVE_AFTER_TRANSFER` means removing an old package after its obligations are transferred; applied to a ledger row it is a stretch of that meaning. One owner rule holds for every row (W00:831): the Owner cell names exactly one stage; another stage a cell names moves to the Resume condition as a consumer. "W00" as an owner means the SPEC-0161 Task's W00 intake record, the workspace's existing Task, not a W00 stage run. The Current owner column shows the owner after the corrections of this package. Result: `REMOVE_AFTER_TRANSFER` 1 (W01-H01); `REVISE` 16 (13 edited here, W00-H01, W03-H02, GOV-04, R147-03, OPEN-01, OPEN-02, OPEN-03, OPEN-04, OPEN-06, W03-H15, W03-H29, SDLC-07 and R45-12, and 3 edited later by SPEC-0211, OPEN-07, DOC-12 and DOC-S99); `KEEP_VALID` 66 (59 open rows, 6 seeds and SPEC-0213); `SUPERSEDE` 0 and `CANCEL_OBSOLETE` 0, so their absence is a result. A `KEEP_VALID` row without a named PKG line is decided by the live row, its recorded decision and W00:831. The eight terminal rows (LEDGER, W03-H08, W03-H16, W03-H27, W03-H30, W03-H33, W03-H36 and W03-H42) are not live obligations and are not classified. The first column is a running number, not a residue ID. The W00 completion test (W00:831: every live obligation has exactly one owner; no deleted package keeps an active link, check input or Task reference) is shown only after the last W03 package: this package claims only the rows it classifies, OPEN-07 is shown to have one owner only after SPEC-0211, and SPEC-0211's closing report reads and re-classifies this table.

The registry `common_forms` record (`stage` `candidate`, `approval_ref` `null`, `content_digest` `sha256:4f79183d59c34ee87d1cab35c5ffc86eb7012f5df1db4dc4b91a8fd412e8c618`, `source_revision` `null` because the v14.7 package is git-ignored) identifies the reviewed bytes only and proves no approval and no equivalence; the repository cannot recompute that digest, which is a stated limit.

| Candidate preparation | recorded | The v14.7 contract file `COMMON-SECTION-CONTRACT.json` and its digest above, re-observed on 2026-11-15; the evidence rows EVD-004 and EVD-028 of this Task that read it; the `common_forms` candidate record in `docs/99.templates/registry.json`. | Who prepared it is stated by no record: `unverified`. |
| Final approval | unverified | None on record. | The contract file states that the common reform and the kept path are a user mandate; that is the source's own authority claim, not an approval record bound to the edition's content, revision and digest. |
| Local adoption | unverified until the criterion decision of SPEC-0209 | The integration check of the SPEC-0209 Task, the semantic review and the user's decision. | Scope: the source roles only (all 16 source roles have a registry form at `2499646f`, beside 11 local forms), local forms excluded, and for ADR new ADRs only. #### 3.0.1 Verdicts

Added 2026-11-15 under SPEC-0216 (EVD-132). These rows are analysis for the stage owners. They are not residue rows, not a progress record, and they accept nothing. The first column is a running number, never a residue ID. Each row has one owner stage. Carried by names the live row or the PKG prompt through which that stage takes the row in at its own intake. No ledger entry is added. `REPLACE` and `REMOVE` have no row. A row read only through PKG's observation is `UNVERIFIED`. No row lowers a requirement to match the tree. Historical audit questions (OPEN-01 to OPEN-04, OPEN-06 and W00-H01) are not reactivated.

Design summary (PKG W00-DELTA). Designs D00, D15 and D17, edition 3.0.1, with external items S03 to S07, S10, S20 to S22, S24 and S27 read for their applicability and limits. Choice: W00 records verdicts and owners only; each `CHANGE` is fixed by its owning stage under the user's requirements. Rejected alternatives: seeding ledger rows, which duplicates the stage prompts that already carry each item; fixing the documents here, which is outside W00's write boundary; and re-running W03. Interface: these tables, which the stage owners read at their own intake. Failure: a PKG hash that differs from `manifest.json` stops the intake. Risk: a verdict being read as acceptance; each row therefore states its read scope. Acceptance test: SPEC-0216 VAL-W00D-001. The two input archives of `input-provenance.json` are absent, so their own analysis is not repeated; PKG's derived files are used instead.

#### W01 3.0.1 Rule Fit

Added 2026-11-15 under SPEC-0217 (EVD-133). This is the W01-CONTRACT rule-fit record against PKG 3.0.1 and its confirmed decisions. It is analysis, not a residue table and not a progress record. The first column is a running number, never a residue ID. Each row names one owning stage, which takes the row in at its own intake under its own PKG prompt; no ledger entry is added.

How each rule's purpose, owner, consumer and enforcement are explained: purpose by the rule's body text; owner by its policy file; consumer and enforcement by the Evidence column of its Rules table. A named check is a mechanical control, and `Semantic review` is not mechanically enforced. The 218 rules of the ten governance policies all carry these, and this table judges their fit only.

Limits: verdicts are text-level. Three read-only subagents read every rule, this session spot-read the duplicates and the overstated findings, and the evidence checks named in the Rules tables were not run. GIT-R012, GIT-R013, FMT-R002, FMT-R003 and FMT-R010 were found to be links or permitted mirrors and are `KEEP_VERIFIED`. GIT-R011 (one approval per commit or push) is kept, because Q08 removes repeated approval of edits, not of outward delivery.

#### QA Latency Record

Added 2026-11-15 under SPEC-0220 (EVD-141). This is the actual latency comparison that the result section of each PKG 3.0.1 stage prompt asks for. It holds measurements only and no rule; reducing the latency is W05-GATES's (U27). The first column is a running number.

| No. | Part | Case | Measurement | Reading |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | Commit QA | Nothing staged | `scripts/qa.py staged`: 0 gates, 13.8 s | Fixed cost of every commit run (index snapshot and setup) |
| 2 | Commit QA | One sentence in `docs/90.references/README.md` | 6 gates, 64.4 s, PASS | An ordinary document commit costs about one minute |
| 3 | Commit QA | One sentence in `.agents/governance/model-selection.md` | 9 gates, 69.3 s, PASS | Three more gates than row 2 added about 5 s |
| 4 | Commit QA | One sentence in this Task | 7 gates, 69.9 s, PASS | The ledger gate is the added gate |
| 5 | Full run | The 16 registered checks of the `W01 Follow-up Record`, run once | About 1,198 s; 15 rc 0, 1 rc 1 | 17 to 19 times one commit run |
| 6 | Full run | Its two largest parts | `document` suite 774.9 s, `qa-runner` suite 316.7 s, 91 percent of the total | None of rows 2 to 4 selected them, so commit QA stays near one minute; this is the observed basis for not running the full suite on every Git event |
| 7 | Limits | Sampling | One run per case, on one host, with no warm-up control | Not a stable benchmark; no mean or spread |
| 8 | Limits | Lanes | Local only; hosted CI time not measured | A hosted run has a different cost |
| 9 | Limits | Coverage | Changes under `scripts/` or `tests/` not measured | They may select the contract suites and take much longer |
| 10 | Limits | Full-run input | The last eight checks of row 5 started after edits began, as the `W01 Follow-up Record` states | They may have read a tree in mid-edit; the same validators passed again on the edited tree |

#### W05-GATES Recheck (4.2.0)

The 2026-11-18 request applies the 4.2.0 W05-GATES prompt with the same
limits, starting from merged `000238cd`. The package marks this task reported
complete, so only differences new in 4.1.0 and 4.2.0 are in scope, together
with the residue that names W05 as its next actor. The designs read were D04,
D12, D15, D25 and D26. No attached proposal disposition targets W05-GATES.

**Call graph and findings.** The commit `pre-commit` hook runs `qa.py
staged` on the exact index. The registry classifies each path into exactly one
surface, and the bounded runner invokes each selected gate. The checks found
the following:

- *Exact required set (D04 4.1.0).* `qa.py` refused a missing or invalid
  required result but not a result for an unplanned gate. It now refuses a
  result for a gate outside the planned set.
- *Repeated execution (QUAL-R001).* Only `document-contract-registry` carried
  a reuse contract. A commit after a direct `qa.py staged` on the same index
  therefore replayed every other document gate, including the
  `document` suite. The reuse identity now also binds the Asia/Seoul date,
  because `validate-markdown-profiles.py` reads today's date, and its version
  is 4. Six gates that are pure Python over the repository bytes, Git and that
  date now opt in: the `document` and `document-lifecycle` suites, document
  lifecycle, the residue ledger, links and Markdown profiles. Gates that run a
  tool outside the identity, such as selected style, kubeconform or conftest,
  stay outside reuse.
- *Selector gap.* `tests/test_validation_tooling_ownership.py` checks every
  test and script, for example that every declared test class is collected.
  It ran only when the routing files changed. Its collection check also
  followed local bases one level deep, so it misreported four classes added in
  `714de75e` whose base is two levels from `TestCase`. The module is now the
  `tooling-ownership` suite and the `tooling-ownership-tests` gate. All 19
  surfaces that classify a test, a script or `.pre-commit-config.yaml` select
  it, and it left the `validation-routing` suite, so nothing runs twice. The
  check now follows local bases at any depth.
- *No duplicate leaves (D04 4.2.0).* No two gates share an argv, and no test
  module sits in two suites.
- *Short document edit.* A one-sentence runbook edit selects seven document
  and style gates and no regression suite.

**Measurement.** Both trees were clones on this host with Python 3.12.3 and
the pinned venv. The input was the same one-sentence RUN-0008 edit, and
`qa.py staged` ran twice on the same index, N=1 per variant. On `main` the
runs took 68.4 s and 66.4 s, and the second reused only the registry gate. On
this change they took 77.1 s and 28.5 s, and the second reused five document
gates. The first-run difference is within single-run noise.

#### W05 Residue Processing (4.2.0)

On 2026-11-18 the user asked to process the W05 residue that was waiting for its own trigger
(`The other W05 residue waits for its own trigger 처리`). Four read-only
investigations mapped each row against the current code. Four implementers
then changed disjoint files. Each behavior change was shown failing first and
then passing.

- **DOC-S90: Closed.** A core `timestamp` or `date` cell must be an ISO date or exactly `not stated in this document` (`core_cell_faults`, BODY-CORE-TABLE-CELL). Nine Checked At cells in research m0016, m0023 to m0026 now hold the latest stated date, and the earlier date or the per-row spread moved to Limits. Source and Version were already required core cells. Freshness stays review, because no age limit is set.
- **DOC-S99: Closed.** `tests/form_headings.py` builds fixture bodies from the registry form tree and refuses a title the form does not register. Builders in ten modules use it, and the converted output is byte-identical except one policy fixture that named an unregistered `### Enforcement`. Old-form, retained-form and arbitrary-heading fixtures keep literals with a reason. Registry pins in `tests.test_common_form_tree` stay. A new `form-heading-fixtures` surface selects the `document`, `document-lifecycle` and `tooling` suites.
- **SDLC-ROWS: Closed.** `TASK-PROMOTION-RECEIPT` refuses a completed Task whose Promotion Receipt omits a Promotion ID of its Plan, or whose Target Owner link names a missing anchor. The completed SPEC-0161, SPEC-0221 and SPEC-0222 Tasks pass. Evidence-text matching stays review, and DOCLIFE-R002 names the check.
- **W05-H04: Closed.** `agent-governance` already refuses provider agent projections, provider baselines, skill links and the Codex rules digest changed alone. AUTHOR-R020's check column now names it for those surfaces and keeps semantic review for other copies.
- **W05-H05: Closed, with W05-H10.** Approval-text contradictions are refused by tests. The scoped receipt now also has a wrong-actor case, which the validator already refused. The one-change-set rule SDLC-R034 is semantic review only, and becomes W05-H10.
- **W05-H06: Closed.** The executable-reference terminal statuses are built from the registry lifecycle domains, which adds `retired`, `resolved` and `published`. ADR `accepted` is kept deliberately.
- **W05-H08: Closed.** One test was renamed to name its passing and failing cases. Four relations now name a real refusal test as `negative_test`, two of them new. `verification-evidence` gained a passing case. The unused `section` carrier and its branch are gone, and `relation.kind` is an enum equal to `RELATION_KIND_IDS`. The consumer paths and gap text were already current.
- **W05-H09: Closed.** Removed: the uncalled `empty_required_h2_sections` and three provenance-string assertions on `migration.meaning_map`. The CI fixture no longer names the retired `qa.py ci`. The rest has a current consumer or contract and stays: the published-report link alias, the route-refusal tests, CTX-R007, CTX-R014, the readers of registry majors 10 to 21, and the current-default empty-body rule.

On 2026-11-18 the user asked to continue with two points from that report (`이어서 계속 진행한다. The m0026 source group, Building the terminal status set`):

- **m0026 dates.** The core row for the source register keeps 2026-08-18, the latest date in that register, which covers SRC-WERPC-001 to 090. The row's ID now names that range. The later dated sections had no core row. Four rows now take them from the document's own text: SRC-WERPC-091 on 2026-08-20, 092 to 115 on 2026-08-23, 116 to 122 on 2026-08-28, and 123 to 154 on 2026-09-05.
- **Terminal statuses.** The registry is the single owner of state meaning. It marks `retired`, the incident `resolved` and the postmortem `published` as terminal, so a script reference in such a document now takes the Git-recovery route instead of EXECUTABLE-CURRENT. This relaxes a check only for documents whose body is no longer kept current. A `deprecated` document stays current, and its references must still resolve. ADR `accepted` stays on the historical route because accepted ADR bodies are frozen (EVD-186).
- **Secrets baseline.** The new m0026 rows moved four lines that `detect-secrets` flags as `Secret Keyword`. The audited baseline held these lines only for the predecessors m0012 and m0007, not for their T99 copies m0026 and m0021, so the copies also failed on `main` (reproduced at `000238cd`). `.secrets.baseline` now carries the same audited entries under each copy's path and current line.

On 2026-11-18 the user asked to finish the remaining items (`남아 있는 항목들에 대해 진행하여 완료하고`). W05-H10 is closed (EVD-187). SDLC-R034 forbids two things: editing a test alone to make a violation pass, and relaxing a requirement by editing a downstream document alone. Both turn on the reason for the change. A path or diff rule cannot separate them from legitimate test-only changes, of which this record has three: the W05-H09 assertion removals, the DOC-S99 fixture refactor, and the selection-pin updates for a new gate. A rule simple enough to check mechanically would refuse all three, which D04 counts as false failure. The validation registry maps paths to gates, not tests to their producer owners, so the condition the row waited for cannot occur. The policy already sets SDLC-R034's enforcement to semantic review, and the quality policy's independent review applies to high-risk changes. The rule therefore stays semantic review, and no check was added.


#### W06-DELIVERY Recheck (4.2.0)

The 2026-11-18 request applies the 4.2.0 W06-DELIVERY prompt from merged
`4af2b30e`, limited to that prompt. The designs read were D04, D05, D17 and
D25. No attached proposal disposition targets W06-DELIVERY. The open W06
residue was CI-SCOPE, OLD-RULE-TESTS, COMMIT-AFTER, MERGE-AFTER, PUSH-BEFORE
and R147-06.

**Decision.** CI-SCOPE needed the operator to accept ADR-0063 and to amend
REQ-0003 FR-0017. Asked on 2026-11-18, the user chose `Accept now`: accept
ADR-0063 and amend FR-0017 now, based on the implemented workflow and the
observed aggregate runs, and keep the selected-leaf hosted observation as
RUNTIME_ONLY residue for the first real gitops/ PR.

**Changes.**

- ADR-0063 is accepted at 1.0.0. Its Authority, Impacts and Follow-up state the acceptance, and its lineage amends only ADR-0048's hosted validation clause.
- REQ-0003 FR-0017 states the need and acceptance (6.1.0, a MINOR change because the implemented mechanism still satisfies it). The exact merge, both-parent and reviewed-base mechanism moved to AD-0006 (3.2.0). The ADR index, QUAL-R006 and SDLC-R016 drop the pending wording.
- `git.md` GIT-R016 and its Git-point binding table name, for commit, push, pull request and merge, the check before, during and after, where it runs and its evidence lane:
  - *Commit.* The installed pre-commit and commit-msg chain runs on the exact staged index, and no post-commit hook exists (COMMIT-AFTER).
  - *Push.* The pre-push dispatcher runs an installed workspace or global hook and otherwise drains its input. Ref protection belongs to the GitHub `main` protection and the provider deny rules (PUSH-BEFORE).
  - *Pull request.* The hosted `ci-summary` and `style-pr` run on the cumulative change.
  - *Merge.* The push run on `main` reports `NOT_APPLICABLE`. No post-merge regression lane is adopted, because strict protection already checked the exact merge with both parents and a replay would repeat a proven leaf (MERGE-AFTER).

**Hosted measurement.** The six PR runs of 2026-11-18, #297 to #302, show
the following times:

| Job | Queue median | Run median | Run times (s) | Setup (checkout, Python, install) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `style-pr` | 2 s | 39 s | 39, 39, 39, 41, 19, 23 | median 8 s |
| `ci-summary` | 2 s | 6 s | 6, 6, 7, 6, 8, 7 | median 3 s |

The difference comes from the style lint step, which took 9 to 27 seconds
depending on the changed files. Each `style-pr` run executed `Verify
affected check outcomes` with the CI lock and GitOps leaves skipped for
absent paths. No run was cancelled, failed or left pending, and no path
filter hides a required job, because `ci.yml` triggers on every
`pull_request` to `main`.

#### W07-BRIEF Recheck (4.2.0)

The 2026-11-18 request applies the 4.2.0 W07-BRIEF prompt from merged
`5e1536a7`, limited to that prompt. The designs read were D08, D03 and D25.
No attached proposal disposition targets W07-BRIEF. The earlier W07 brief
work added the explicit preflight `scripts/validate-execution-brief.py` and
its tests.

**Differences found.** Two 4.1.0 conditions of D08 were not met:

- *Partial invalidation.* The preflight compared every bound field, including
  all `material_inputs`, and read every material file. A change to any material
  input, such as an evidence-only append to the Task, therefore refused every
  action. D08 4.1.0 invalidates only the affected work.
- *Consumer.* No procedure called the preflight. The delegated-development
  workflow said to recheck the binding on resume but named no check.

**Changes.**

- An action binding in `allowed_actions` may now list the material `inputs` it
  reads. Such an action compares and reads only its target paths and those
  inputs. An action without `inputs` binds every material input, as before, so
  the default stays strict. An unknown input name is refused. Every other bound
  field still binds all actions.
- The delegated-development workflow now requires the preflight before a
  worker mutation handoff, a resume after a correction or compaction, and a
  state change for a worker. A refusal stops only the refused action. A
  read-only or review handoff and a clear low-risk task proceed on the short
  brief, with no per-file re-approval. The workflow states that the preflight
  grants no authority, and that a provider path that does not call it is an
  enforcement gap.
- The `scripts/README.md` usage text documents `inputs`.

**Kept.** The preflight already treats authenticity as the caller's job,
because a brief and a current request written by one author prove nothing.
Questions block only their affected actions. A withdrawn or changed request,
a stale brief revision and a changed HEAD are still refused.
